No agent connected to a device, wrote to one, or deployed.
Previous status, retained: CLEAR AND KEEP ON DEVICE LOOK THE SAME AT REST, AND THE SUMMARY SAYS SO RATHER THAN BURYING IT. A-46 retired the Bare tier before it shipped; DEVICE_COMPONENTS was SIX and is now SEVEN, and removing the name turned ONLY the length assertion red while the 44px walk passed having read nothing - the hand list IS the walk's scope. SAFE-02 survives D-04 as a site-wide count of the accent fill. 80 files / 821 tests, 101 e2e, check 583, sweep 4 19 at 90 s.
Previous status, retained: THE NEGATIVE CHECK FOUND A HOLE IN THE TEST RATHER THAN IN THE CODE, WHICH IS THE SECOND TIME IN THREE WAVES. The plan asked for a forbidden-word scan over the rendered strings and for it to be observed red on a planted metaphor. It was written, it was green, and the plant - Breeds half its settings from each - left it silent, because a scan that asks whether a word list CONTAINS breed cannot see breeds, and would not have seen breeding, parents, mutated or children either. A metaphor arrives inflected far more often than bare. Re-cut to match by stem, the same perturbation reports MIX_LINE says breeds - the mechanism, named where the result should be. That is 10-10's hex lesson and 10-09's U+2212 lesson a third time: a check nobody ran is a check that does not exist, and a check that ran and passed on a real fault is worse, because it is now evidence. Four negative checks in all, all four resolved, every perturbed file restored by inverse edit with sha256 either side and no git checkout, restore, stash or clean at any point. The rest of the wave went where the plan said it would not: mix.ts is fifty lines and took minutes, and the time went on the two things a component cannot demonstrate by being looked at - a property test that counts before it asserts, and a canvas budget reconciled by re-reading the picker rather than by citing the note that recorded it. Two honest gaps are written down rather than papered over: MIX TWO is not mounted, because the SimHost hop is Coverflow's and because 10-UI-SPEC gives the block no term in the region's height arithmetic, which two tests hold; and it renders no disabled reason, because 13.4 gives it four strings and no fifth and a component that writes its own sentence is the drift copy.ts exists to prevent.
Previous status, retained: A PLAN THAT ARRIVED FULLY COMMITTED AND STILL FAILED A GATE, AND EVERY ONE OF ITS FOUR FAULTS WAS A NUMBER RATHER THAN AN OPINION. Both tasks were on disk as commits and all three cheap gates were green; the tuning e2e failed 3 of 20 and a hand probe found a fourth the suite cannot see. The thumb is centred on its value, so at the top detent it paints 6px past the rail - and aurora SHIPS at 0,85,255, so the blue rail stands there on arrival: 251 against 245 on a phone, 342 against 336 on chromium. Knob.svelte has the identical rule and is safe only because its .row grid keeps a lock column to the thumb's right, which is the kind of thing a component learns by being second. The head's four members need 220px of furniture before the knob's own 39px name gets a pixel, so below a 262px container the metadata goes - it is the one member carrying no information of its own, being aria-hidden over the same three integers aria-valuetext announces - and the rule is the LAST in the file because .value's own display: flex would otherwise win on source order, which was observed rather than feared. tuning.e2e.ts's rails() indexed by position and the picker took aurora's first slot, so turnRail(0) and turnRail(1) were turning red and green: that moves a pad and a meter, so eight of ten tests stayed green while RESET ALL compared two identical racks - the failure mode where a helper's assumption rots and only the strictest assertion notices. And a picker with a knob selector cannot be one 44px line at 320px at all: three 44px options plus a 63px caption plus a 44px lock is 263px of min-content against 172px of rack, and the 44px floor is a contract rather than a style, so the block became a floor and the 96px under-reservation is measured, written into both comments and assigned to 10-13.1 with the pill's own 12px-against-24px padding, because every threshold in a proper fix is a function of that number. The five negative checks the interrupted session left no record of were all run here, all red, all reverted by inverse edit with sha256 either side - and the fifth changed the test rather than the report, exactly as 10-09's U+2212 check did: a hex announcement fired an equality with no message, so the scan was moved above it and a red run now names the rule.
Previous status, retained: TWO IDEAS NO EDITOR IN THE CATEGORY HAS, AND THE ONE THAT ARRIVED ALREADY WRITTEN WAS THE ONE THAT WAS BROKEN. Task 1 was inherited as 545 uncommitted lines from a session killed mid-plan: green on check, green on lint, green on 802 quick tests, and RED on the phone project. The lock's column had turned the knob row from a block into a three-column grid, and a grid track's automatic minimum size is its content's min-content width - so the options row refused to wrap and pushed knob-rack to scrollWidth 267 against clientWidth 245, breaking the tuning region's outright prohibition on horizontal scroll in a place only tuning-webkit.e2e.ts looks. Every flexible track is minmax(0, 1fr) now, in four places, with the measurement written beside the rule, and it is 20 of 20. TUNE-04's locks ship with the state in the accessible name (HOLD becomes HELD) and a second channel that spends no colour: the default marker moves from a --color-line-soft dot at the default to a --color-line bar spanning the selected option. The accent census over the seven tuning components is 14 before and 14 after, and it is now an ASSERTION with all eight reserved entries quoted in its failure message rather than a script anybody has to remember to run. A held knob never reaches the rng, so holding all but one collapses the roll's domain and the no-op rejection can finally burn the whole twelve-draw bound - a path that was unreachable before locks existed, now driven by a test that observes exactly 12 draws and 0 offered to the compiler and says in its own comment why it could not have been written last week. A held rack's link is byte-identical to an unheld one, measured off a non-default vector because two undefineds would prove nothing. TUNE-02's forecast is the half nobody had started: hovering or focusing an option now shows what it would cost before it is chosen, as a ghost band in both meters and a signed mono delta beside the option. cost() only and never fit(), and the guard is not new - model.spec.ts has held fitState to one call site since Phase 5, and the negative check that called the fitter from the forecast went red there, with the debounce test NOT blowing its budget, so which guard caught it is a measurement rather than a guess. Memoised on the index vector, so a hit publishes on a microtask and only a miss waits out the same 120 ms a recompile waits: no second timing constant. The ghost is three bands in one span and one token, so a forecast above reads as the extra and a forecast below reads as a notch cut out of a fill that genuinely stops short - and there is no direction branch that could paint the two differently by accident. Never on touch, gated twice because the two gates fail differently. The fifth --font-mono use and the fifth permitted typographic character both arrived with a scope attached: U+2212 appears exactly once in the comment-stripped code of all of src/, asserted, and the spec names the glyph by escape so it cannot defeat its own assertion. --color-over is still exactly three uses and the fourth is deliberately not taken. One gap is counted rather than claimed: the forecast is offered on option rows only, because a rail has no candidate element to hover and no keyboard candidate at all - five of the twelve knob kinds are rails unconditionally - and 10-10's picker is the pointer-to-detent mapping that closes it. 78 files / 804 tests (+0 / +3), 97 e2e (+0), check 577 (+0).
Previous status, retained: THE SWEEP GOT A THIRD BIGGER AND THE WALL CLOCK GOT SMALLER, AND THE PIN THAT NOBODY WAS SUPPOSED TO ASSUME WAS WRONG FIVE TIMES OUT OF SIX. D-06's colour knob is now the whole reachable RGB444 lattice - 4,096 positions, read()/apply() as index arithmetic through the vendored quantiseColour, read(apply(state, i)) === i by construction - and G-07's restructure costs the colour dimension LINEARLY beside a colour-pinned cross-product rather than multiplying by 683. The honest total RISES, 19,502 + 24,576 = 44,078 against 32,852, and the plan says so rather than repeating the spec's first-pass claim that it would fall. THE MEASUREMENT THAT PAID: Pass B runs first and hands Pass A the dearest literal it just measured, and it is 102,102,102 on five of the six colour-bearing presets - position 1,638, the first index whose three channels are all three digits, and every nine-digit literal ties - with 255,255,255 winning only on ninepads, whose checkerboard emits a dimmed second copy. With the pin measured, all nine worst-cost figures reproduce 10-UI-SPEC 11.4 exactly; pinned at the old six-swatch palette's dearest, aurora and radar each read one character high. A-11 is corrected in the suite rather than in prose: tpad is 907 of 908 and has NO colour knob, ninepads at 640 of 908 leaves 268 free and that margin is an EQUALITY so it cannot shrink quietly, and the set of colours the picker would have to disable is asserted EMPTY rather than skipped. SHARE-03's irreversible failure mode was closed in the right order: wild-stamps.json - 54 format x literals from the unmodified encoder - was committed ALONE, one commit before format w existed, and the test asserts both that every captured literal still lands restored AND that 25 of the 27 entries now emit a different format, which is what makes it evidence instead of a tautology. STAMP_OPTION_CEILING stays 32 and the colour knob is exempt BY FORMAT; raising the number was measured breaking exactly two assertions and both are the value pin itself, so the pin is the only guard and that is now written down. THE ONE THING THE LATTICE BROKE, AND IT WAS FOUND BY A TEST RATHER THAN BY A USER: 4,096 radio inputs in the rack overflow and intercept every other control's pointer events, red in 13 of 20 tuning e2e tests, so model.ts shows a two-swatch window with KnobView.positions carrying the real knob index - and the one consumer outside the widget that read .index directly reported window slot 0 for aurora's colour, which disabled KEEP ON DEVICE with knobs-moved after a write nobody had touched. 78 files / 801 tests (+0 / +0), 97 e2e (+0), check 577 (+1), sweep 4 / 19 unchanged at 119 s against BASE_SWEEP_WALL 123 s
Previous status, retained: THE BROWSE VOCABULARY IS NOW RENDERED, AND THE ONE THING THIS WAVE COULD HAVE GOT SILENTLY WRONG WAS PROVED BY AN ASSERTION FAILING IN THE RIGHT DIRECTION. 10-06 declared sixteen terms in two facets; 10-07 puts them on the screen as two captioned facet rows, deletes the count-derived row and the outsider chip that needed it, removes the Newest sort with addedAt's browse projection, and migrates the URL. THE INVERSION IS THE FINDING: browse.e2e.ts's shipped block pressed `playable` then `generative` and asserted their INTERSECTION was smaller than either - true on the fifty-five-term vocabulary, and false now, because both words are FEELS terms and pressing both is a UNION of 21 of 36 against playable's 13. Restoring the old assertion goes red with "Expected < 13, Received 21", which proves both terms landed in FEELS rather than merely that the new assertion passes, and a cross-facet press on `modulation` (4 entries: radar, arc, ghost, shuttle) proves the AND half the union alone would never have reached. G-10 rules FOUR ways an inbound ?tag= can go, not three: mapped becomes a chip, shipped-but-unmapped becomes ?q= (?tag=looper as ?q=looper), NEVER-A-TAG is dropped under W-12 unamended, and an explicit ?q= always wins - the precedence 10-UI-SPEC left open, answered from an observed run rather than guessed. The address is also CANONICALISED once on arrival through the existing 500 ms timer, without which a shared ?tag= link would have kept saying ?tag= for the whole visit. TWO THINGS THE PLAN ASSERTED ARE CORRECTED BY MEASUREMENT: negative check 7's gate does not exist - `npm run check` stays green with browse-webkit.e2e.ts's retired sort restored, because .svelte-kit/tsconfig.json's include globs cover src/, test/ and tests/ and NEVER e2e/, so nothing type-checks the e2e suite and Playwright's esbuild never will; and the FOR link row sits below the fidelity line rather than between it and the name plate, because both belong to Coverflow.svelte, which this phase does not edit. Six e2e sites repaired inside their own titles, no test( line added, removed or renamed - 11 and 3 before and after. 78 files / 801 tests (+0 / -1), 97 e2e (+0), check 576 (+1), sweep untouched by derivation
Previous status, retained: D-09 IS MET FOR EVERY CARD A FINGER CAN REACH, AND THE ONE IT CANNOT REACH IS NAMED IN SOURCE WITH ITS MEASUREMENT RATHER THAN QUIETLY LIT. Three of the four dark configurations - GHOST, MORPH and ETCH - now replay an authored gesture through the EXISTING TouchSampler at the EXISTING one-sample-per-contact-per-tick rate, and they light 14, 19 and 5 cells where frames.json records 0 at all five sampled ticks. HANGAR supplies the finger; the firmware supplies every pixel; no configuration, Lua source, character budget or frame hash moved. THE FOURTH CANNOT BE LIT BY ANYTHING, AND THAT IS THE PHASE-LEVEL FINDING: Trackpad's draft sets look.kind and touch.kind to none and disables both layers, so it lights 0 of 81 cells under a one-finger drag, a two-finger scroll, a single tap, a two-finger tap and 2,000 idle ticks - measured, five ways, on the shipped engine - and front-door.ts:62 has said 'it writes no LEDs at all' since Phase 4. Giving it a picture means turning on a look it does not have, which is 10-UI-SPEC 9.3's option (a), which that table REJECTS by name, so the card stays black and says so in its own sentence. FOUR HOST CHANGES, ONE OF WHICH THE SPEC DID NOT ASK FOR: delivery for demo entries; a widened freeze test; stillFrame()'s second branch (reset, replay once, freeze); and ONE TouchSampler PER DEMO ENTRY rather than a bigger MAX_CONTACTS - measured, on a shared sampler, at three of the visitor's five fingers accepted and ZERO of them delivered to the hero, every accepted sample landing on the first demo card's engine instead. The unasked-for change is active()'s third term: the term the spec names stops the loop between two gestures and NOTHING but a tick can restart it, so two of the three cards would have played once and frozen. R-10 retired RESTS_DARK_NOTE and kept restsBlack with a second job - it selects a demo path, asserted in both directions - and gen-og.mjs's non-dark gate lost its restsBlack exemption BY NAME, because a black image for a restsBlack entry would still have passed, which is the exact regression this wave exists to prevent. ETCH's 4,192-byte black OG square is 4,737 bytes; static/og/ is 213,919 over 36 files against 210,926, byte-identical across two builds. Coverflow.svelte byte-untouched, and provably so: no demo entry can mount there, because tpad is in EXCLUDED_FROM_ROW and the other three are preview lua, which front-door.spec.ts:110-112 excludes. 77 files / 798 tests (+1 / +5), 97 e2e (+0), sweep 4 19 unchanged.
Previous status, retained: THE CRT SHIPPED, AND THE TWO THINGS IT WAS SENT TO PROVE WERE BOTH PROVED BY BEING WRONG FIRST. 10-02 left one instruction: re-run its negative check 4 and report whether the new scans turn it red. They do - moving --crt-scanline into a component now names the file - but the scan 10-UI-SPEC 8.7 actually specifies would NOT have caught it, because PadFrame.svelte is on the very allowlist the move exploits. So scan 1 carries a half the spec did not ask for: 7.1's placement rule, every --crt property in the one file identity.spec.ts reads, --crt-noise the one exception asserted from both sides, no colour literal in any CRT rule outside it. The second thing: the noise tile's home was decided by RUNNING the gate rather than by reasoning about it, and the gate said green twice - once with the tile in app.css and once with a pure red hidden inside the tile in app.css. A percent-encoded hue is no hex at all to that walk. Green from a gate that provably cannot see the thing is uninformative, so the tile went into the component and scan 6 is what holds it. FOUR LAYERS, NONE OF THEM ABLE TO REACH A WORD: G behind all content, S a leaf inside a slot, R and T bound to .band's box through a shell that is a SIBLING of the whole Coverflow output, authored in FrontDoor.svelte because Coverflow.svelte may not be edited - and is not, byte for byte, across all three commits. Scan 3 walks .crt-band's own subtree and asserts it is empty of words; scan 7 reads five geometry literals out of both files and names both on a difference. TWO MEASUREMENTS, ONE OF WHICH CONTRADICTS THE SPEC. Layer S on thirty-six browse frames costs 0.00 ms at p95 in chromium and 61 ms on webkit at a phone viewport, thirty times 8.5's threshold, so the declared fallback applies and Layer S is the front door's seven frames only - gated in the browser, because a fallback that ships without a gate is a comment. And Layer G, which 8.5 records as zero by construction, was costing 105 ms per scrolled frame through a 26vmax inset blur re-rastered every frame; the vignette is a gradient now and the layer is promoted, 196 ms down to 101 against a 78 ms floor, chromium unmoved at 16.7 throughout. The residual - a 22 ms halftone on one engine on a harness already at 13 fps - is decomposed and deferred rather than fixed by scoping the site's ground texture off its busiest page on a benchmark artefact. THE OFF SWITCH IS ONE ATTRIBUTE AND THREE SWITCHES, ALL THREE ASSERTED IN BOTH BROWSER PROJECTS. The reduced-motion gate reads animationName at the compositor - which the shipped canvas-backing-store assertions structurally cannot see - and it means something only because the tear's animation is declared AT REST AND PAUSED: without that it would read none with and without the preference and assert nothing. One deviation is worth carrying forward on its own: a shell heredoc halved a double backslash and turned a word boundary into a backspace character, and the check it was in matched nothing while reporting that an element was missing. Long content goes through the Write tool.
Previous status, retained: THE HARD HALF OF DELETING TEXT IS THAT EVERY NAMED STRING HAS A SIZING TWIN, AND THIS PLAN SPENT A MEASURED NUMBER RATHER THAN ADJUSTING ONE. Nine of the phase's ten retirements landed - 969 characters retired, 356 written back, 618 net gone from the site, every one of those figures produced by a script reading the tree before and after rather than by eye. SAFE-01 is the delicate one and it shipped as the spec's recommended form: SAFE_NOTE, `Nothing is written without a click.`, 35 characters, Micro (title) in --color-ink at 9.26:1, 8px beneath the primary, UNCONDITIONAL and never a sizing twin, rendered on the chosen panel in every state - writing, all seven failure blocks, and a browser that cannot write at all - and beneath the header's device slot in all seven states the note renders in. That is strictly MORE than the 88-character paragraph it replaces ever reached: SAFE_PROMISE was in three of the nine slot states and inside a <details> a visitor had to open. Both reversal forms are costed in the SUMMARY at four files each. THE MEASUREMENT BOUND TWO SHIPPED SENTENCES AND NEITHER CAP MOVED: HONESTY_CAP is 2 x 43 = 86 rather than 129, and HONESTY_READY (contract form 90) and tryOnBudgetReason's worst form (90) were both shortened to 85, because a cap widened to admit its own string stops reserving anything. HONESTY_READY's amendment is asserted from BOTH sides - the contract's 90-character form must be present in an approved contract AND over the cap - so deleting the row is red on the shipped string and faking it is red on the contract's. The five reservations are formulas with 43 substituted: header note 152px -> 24px, honesty slot 72px -> 48px, PUT BACK 72px and KEEP 48px unchanged under a new face and re-derived anyway, and the meters block's 56px shown to be arithmetic over line boxes and gaps with no character count in it at all - ChosenPanel.svelte and tune-ui.spec.ts:344 byte-unchanged, which git and sed both confirm. Zero test counts moved (76 / 787 in and out, e2e 89, check 570 after the one deleted component). Two findings worth carrying: PUT BACK's headroom is 28 characters and the cap IS the line boundary, so the cap test is the cell's only guard; and negative check 3 came back red where the plan predicted green, which was resolved by running it a second way - with the new absence assertion removed, the whole quick suite is green while a retired user-facing string sits re-exported and unrendered, so neither copy module enumerates its exports and 10-12 should close that when it adds CLEAR's strings
Previous status, retained: THE TYPE SETTLEMENT SHIPPED AS A STRUCTURE RATHER THAN AS A PROMISE, AND ITS TWO MOST IMPORTANT RESULTS ARE A GREEN TEST AND AN EIGHTH EDIT. The display face is reachable through exactly one custom property and exactly one @font-face block, and identity.spec.ts test 7 asserts --font-display's first family equals the font-family of EVERY display @font-face in the file - every, not the second - so the assertion survives the D-14 route the site ships, the A-02 reversal and any later two-face arrangement without being rewritten. That was proved by pointing --font-display at "Archivo" while the face stayed "Grifter" and watching test 7 name both strings. The green result is the finding that matters: --crt-scanline moved out of app.css into PadFrame.svelte's <style> leaves identity.spec.ts at 7 passed AND aesthetic.spec.ts green, which is exactly the silent hole UI-SPEC 7.1's placement rule exists to close and which nothing in the tree closes until 10-04 adds its six scans - 10-VALIDATION:486 expects aesthetic red there and it is not, yet. The widened rgb() regex did not widen the ladder: --color-crt inside @theme is still red on the tenth token while rgb(0 0 0 / 0.5) is a permitted literal, and #0a0a0b is still red on the fourth hue. The archive mechanism was corrected as well as implemented - the tarball is written by postbuild.mjs with git archive HEAD, not by deploy.mjs as UI-SPEC 16 and 18 say, which is why the exclusion cannot be tested from the working tree at all; procedure (a) was run, a scratch commit produced an archive of 335 entries and 1,246 KB carrying hangar-bf20d13/static/fonts/GRIFTER-Bold.woff2 against the real 334 and 1,230 KB, and git reset --hard put it back with .gitattributes byte-identical by sha256 and zero SCRATCH commits reachable from any ref. deploy.mjs step 5 now refuses on a font binary AND on a missing static/fonts/README.md, both predicates exercised on real listings, and npm run deploy was not run. Quicksand left the tree, node_modules, licenses/ and THIRD-PARTY.md's hand-written paragraph in one commit; Grifter is recorded by family, licensee and licence name with no SPDX identifier and no licences/ file, and gen-licenses.mjs:41's allowlist was deliberately not touched because its own contract is that it is extended in a commit with a reason. The plan's allowlist row does not type-check and its exportIgnored field was dropped rather than the interface widened - a record free to disagree with the file it describes is worse than no record. Two commits are red on one test by construction and both are stated: 86f89e7 leaves identity.spec.ts red on the two assertions 7fdeb7d widens, and 7fdeb7d leaves font-assets.spec.ts red on the binary 86f89e7 tracked, which is 10-01's Wave 0 gate catching an unplanted violation and the best evidence in the plan that it was worth writing. Quick 76 / 787 + 1 todo (PREV_FILES + 0, PREV_TESTS + 1), e2e 89 re-measured rather than assumed because Inter is 2.35 per cent wider than Quicksand, svelte-check 571 / 0 / 0, lint clean, src/vendor untouched, Coverflow.svelte in no diff.
Previous status, retained: WAVE 0 IS MEASUREMENT AND THE MEASUREMENT MOVED TWO NUMBERS. CH_PER_LINE is 43, not the provisional 46, and the premise behind 46 is refuted rather than missed: Inter is 2.35 per cent WIDER than Quicksand at 16px (673.39px against 657.95px on the 86-character CLEAR line), takes more line boxes than Quicksand on two of the twenty-eight Body sentences in the copy contract and fewer on none. It was measured as the minimum occupancy of a full line box - a Range per character bucketed by its top - over thirty-six full lines, because the plan's own instrument returns the character count on a block element and a ragged average once corrected. All five of the spec's sizing reservations survive intact; only HONESTY_CAP moves, 129 to 86, and the consequence is copy rather than layout: HONESTY_READY and tryOnBudgetReason's worst form are each 90 against a cap of 86 and must shorten by four, and CLEAR_LINE sits exactly on 86 with no headroom. FONT_SRC is candidate (b), the relative url() into node_modules, and it is proved at BOTH depths - the probe route's own, and src/app.css's by a perturbation reverted byte-identical - because a specifier proved only where a probe sits is not proved where the shipped file will sit: 200, font/woff2, 48,256 bytes, and the un-fingerprinted path 404s. BASE_SWEEP_WALL is 123 s at 4 19, taken before 10-08 grows the sweep by a third and unrecoverable afterwards. The two gates landed green with three negative checks observed red, one of them unasked: font-assets.spec.ts walks git ls-files rather than the working directory and both its matchers prove they fire, because with an empty allowlist expect.requireAssertions caught two tests making no assertion at all; aesthetic.spec.ts scan 4 matches on the rightmost compound of a selector and on a declaration's property name, so Coverflow.svelte:1013 - .stage.measured .slot with filter and opacity in its transition - stays legal, which is the trap V-01 exists to prevent. The plan's non-vacuity floor of twenty rules was false against a file that parses into twelve. Inter is a production dependency, not -D: /dev/type/ is prerendered and deployed, so the woff2 is in the artefact, and licence-notices.spec.ts said so by going red. Quick 74 / 780 -> 76 / 786, sweep 4 19 unchanged, e2e 89 unchanged, svelte-check 567 -> 571 at 0 / 0, src/app.css byte-unchanged and src/vendor untouched.
Previous status, retained: THE COST OF THIRTY-SIX IS MEASURED RATHER THAN PROJECTED, and four of the sixteen rows disagree with the research by more than ten per cent. The build did not grow at all (12.08 s against 12 s at sixteen entries) and the sweep came in 36 % cheaper than projected — 701 combinations over twenty-seven entries, not ~1,090 — because all seven Phase 8 entries carry a sixteen-value channel knob and none of the twenty does. Both .wasm assets are byte-identical, so twenty more Lua entries cost exactly zero bytes of WebAssembly. Thirty-six canvases mount and FOUR are on screen at 1280x720, which the repository had already measured at sixteen entries and nobody had put together: the catalog changes only the mounted count. The phase's two baselines meet here — quick 73 + 1 files and 776 + 4 tests, sweep 3 13 -> 4 19, e2e 89 unchanged — and the chain 776 - 1 + 5 + 0x7 = 780 is why 09-02 says +5 and this says +4 with neither wrong. Three load-sensitive tests were found by running the commands rather than by any gate and all three were fixed without sampling or trimming anything: a per-pixel template string over 27.2 million pixels, a sweep with one second of headroom against a 5,000 ms default, and three fixed 5 ms waits where the test meant a condition. The audition reads as one document with the six rows that matter named first and split into the family the simulator deliberately cannot reproduce and the family whose output nothing consumes. Eight deferred items, including the phase's quietest finding: kind "state" was preferred by D-06 and used zero times, and a state entry would still ship with no knobs and no shareable link. NOTHING ABOUT THE TWENTY CONFIGURATIONS IS CLAIMED AS HARDWARE-VERIFIED.
Previous status, retained: The slate is complete. THIRTY-SIX catalog entries, twenty-seven of them hand-authored Lua, TWENTY authored in this phase, and ZERO of source.kind "state" — the headline finding of the phase, stated as a number rather than as prose. QUADRANT is the accessibility card and its claim was measured rather than asserted: the tick-0 frame reduced to max(r,g,b) gives four distinct patterns at 16 / 8 / 12 / 4 lit cells of sixteen, drawn from a four-entry mask table (65535, 23130, 63903, 33825), and the finding inside the measurement is that HUE SEPARATION IS NOT LUMINANCE SEPARATION — on the palette actually named "high contrast", chartreuse and cyan sit at a luminance ratio of 1.02:1 and are the same colour on one channel, so the fill is the only thing telling those two quadrants apart. Eighteen scripted taps along the dead cross produced ZERO MIDI messages and four cells inside one quadrant produced note 48 four times. Setup 835 (the largest in the catalog) / no Timer, 838 at the corner, 70 free, 17 combinations. POMODORO is the phase's longest-lived card and the only one that meets the 655 second glt ceiling: its Timer re-issues glpfs and glt on all 49 inner cells every 300 seconds against a 60000-tick timeout, the branch sits OUTSIDE the running test so a finished pomodoro still breathes, and 160,020 ticks through createLuaHost left the centre cell at rate 1 and timeout 49,979 with the frames twenty ticks apart still differing — 0 errors and exactly two MIDI messages, one alarm, once. Ring 32 lit at tick 37 and 31 at tick 1009; 400 s of paused time moved it by zero cells. Setup 733 / Timer 647, 735 / 649 at the corner, 173 free, 22 combinations. TWO DEVIATIONS, both Rule 1 and both found by gates the plan's own verify blocks do not name: POMODORO produced NO output at all inside lua-smoke's 218-tick window because its only note is twenty-five minutes away, so every tap now sends a transport note an octave below the alarm; and QUADRANT's four-colour palette declared kind "colour", whose swatch widget needs one RGB triple per value — knobs.lua.spec.ts makes that fall-through a hard failure — so the knob ships kind "mode" and the loss is deferred item 5. The phase-wide sweep over the finished LISTING: 19 non-animated entries, every one carrying a non-empty single-line quiet sentence, four restsBlack entries all carrying RESTS_DARK_NOTE byte-for-byte, 36 distinct descriptions all inside the 110 cap, correct tag counts everywhere, and ZERO disagreements between a declared motion or restsBlack and the fixture. Chip row 26 -> 28: accessible and calm both cross, readable takes the head back from playable at eleven against ten, KNOWN_TAGS unmoved at 55 — the first entry wave of the phase that coins no tag. The full e2e suite was re-measured in both projects at --workers 3: 89 passed, BASE_E2E + 0 across the whole phase, with twenty new prerendered pages under it, because every e2e count derives from LISTING.length. Quick unmoved at 74 / 780 + 1 todo, sweep 4 19 at 701 combinations, build 36 OG images and 36 prerendered pages, svelte-check 567 / 0 / 0.
Previous status, retained: The catalog plays snake, holds a drawing and runs Conway’s Life, and none of the three needs a ZONA to be worth watching. Thirty-four entries, twenty-five of them hand-authored Lua. SNAKE is the phase’s most shareable card and it plays ITSELF: without an autopilot the deterministic snake travels in a straight line forever and never dies, so the plan’s own "runs, dies and restarts" could not have held — a perpendicular-only greedy costs 121 characters, cannot reverse by construction, and yields five bites and a death every 6.5 seconds, identically, forever. The torus step needed correcting too: a single signed index step cannot wrap horizontally, because recovering the row step needs step//9, which floor-divides -1 to MINUS ONE and would have made a left-steered snake climb a row every step, silently. ETCH is the catalog’s third dark card and its OG image is a genuine black square at 4,192 bytes, the smallest in the directory; its whole mechanism is the glt that is NOT there, marked do-not-add, and the wipe was measured firing on a jump strictly greater than the threshold at all four settings. LIFE computes into a second buffer and swaps, caps births at the literal six because twelve messages is 168 of the 256-byte cycle buffer and an append that does not fit is refused with no error, and re-seeds its glider when two consecutive hashes repeat. One more finding: glp after a decay is a bug, not a repaint — it writes the phase and leaves the rate, so a LIFE cell coming back from the dying layer would keep rate 247 and fade straight out; glpfs(a,l,255,0,0) is the correct call. Three frames.json regenerations, all byte-identical. Both determinism checks byte-identical at tick 1009. `game` and `still` both cross into the chip row at twenty-six chips, and `playable` takes the head back from `readable` on the name tiebreak at ten each. 74 files / 780 passed unmoved; sweep 4 19 at 662 combinations.
Previous status, retained: The catalog rates a photograph, runs a build and finds a window, and all three say on the card that HANGAR cannot show a keystroke arriving. Thirty-one entries, twenty-two of them hand-authored Lua, and the still half of the catalog has drawn level at fourteen animated and fourteen static. CULL is five rating bands whose fills — solid, a checker, three pips, two edge pips, one centre pip — were reduced to one channel and measured distinct BEFORE the card claimed they were, because a card that claims shape and ships two identical fills is worse than one that claims only colour. FORGE is twenty-seven macros in three colour families with a second bank under a held corner, and both dropped-release mitigations ship and were watched firing: the release keys off the CONTACT ID rather than the cell, so a lift at (10,10) still returns the bank, and the watchdog Timer is armed by the corner and by nothing else — which is what lets a required Timer coexist with an honest `static`, because a one-shot nobody arms leaves timerArmed false at rest. SWITCH is nine 3x3 marks held as nine integers, minimum pairwise difference two lit cells, and it is the most lit card in the catalog at 243 of 243 non-zero bytes. Two findings worth carrying: the pinned minifier RESPACES `&` and `^` and leaves `>>` alone, so a stored bit mask must be a shift and a modulo; and a deliberately green negative check showed that nothing in this repository would notice if the nine glyphs were eight — only the frame hash moved, and re-taking the fixture made the whole suite green with a card promising nine marks and drawing eight. That is deferred item 3.
Previous status, retained: The catalog picks a colour, switches a scene and scrubs a timeline, and two of the three are honest that HANGAR cannot show them working. Twenty-eight entries, nineteen of them hand-authored Lua. LUMEN is eighty-one distinct computed colours painted once and then nothing runs at all — a nine-anchor hue table across, a 36-minus-row-times-depth ramp down, an amber-white ninth column because a lighting picker with no white is missing its first choice, and 171 of 243 non-zero bytes, third in the catalog and the only entry that lights every cell. STAGE is nine countable boxes and one gks call per press, ten arguments so firmware accepts it, on usage ids read off the USB HID table rather than remembered. SHUTTLE is the ring whose spin IS the speed, reversing with the transport through the uint8 phase wrap, stopping when your finger leaves. The wave's real finding is about honesty in two directions: gks is recorded and inert so a keyboard card animates perfectly and proves nothing — which is now in the card copy, the headers, bench rows 23 and 24 and deferred item 2 — and a declared motion is a promise about the picture, not a reading of the engine, which is why SHUTTLE's ring was given real motion instead of the declaration being bent to fit.
Previous status, retained: The catalog mixes, fades in fourteen bits and helps a DAW learn. Twenty-five entries, sixteen of them hand-authored Lua. CONSOLE is a mixer whose mute is a latch the compiler has no word for and it is honest about not being a Mackie surface; STRIP spends the whole pad on one number and says exactly what the browser can and cannot see of the fourteen-bit pair; LEARN is built on soloStream, the one idea the compiler has and deliberately refuses to persist. The chip row moved by six in a single wave and KNOWN_TAGS did not move at all. deferred-items.md exists and holds the Mackie question.
Previous status, retained: The catalog refuses a wrong note, confirms a fired clip and draws the wave it is asking for. Twenty-two entries, thirteen of them hand-authored Lua. KEYS is the in-key half of the argument LATTICE makes the other half of: LATTICE is chromatic and asks you to learn a geometry, KEYS removes the notes that geometry would let you get wrong - Setup turns a semitone list into a twelve-slot lookup in one ipairs pass, stores a note per in-scale cell in self.k, and paints roots on both layers, the scale on one at phase 96, and nothing at all out of key, so self.k is the membership test as well as the note map and a press on a dark cell returns before it sends anything (observed as zero MIDI and a byte-identical frame). GRIDLOCK is eighty-one launch cells over nine countable track blocks, and its ring is a receipt rather than decoration: the Chebyshev distance from the fired cell becomes a starting phase, every cell climbs to exactly 256 and wraps to black on its own derived timeout, so the bright edge walks outward and the layer lands on exact black at every knob setting - which matters because there is no Timer to repaint a stranded ring. TABLE draws four wave shapes from arithmetic and redraws only when the shape index changes, because touch_cb has a Lua-time budget the LED writes do not. Five deviations, every one a measured correction to the plan's specified mechanism: a knob's values are also a display vocabulary and the cheapest Lua was the wrong literal; +y runs down so a note grid written the plan's way plays lower as the hand goes up; a row interval of twelve makes the plan's own range rule unsatisfiable; a monotone shape couples brightness to lifetime so a staggered decay cannot make an expanding ring; and nine velocities of one to nine are legal and inaudible. All three classify static and all three carry the quiet line their entry landed with, exactly as the plan predicted. The chip row is sixteen at twenty-two entries and the bench checklist is eighteen rows. Next: 09-05 (CONSOLE, STRIP and LEARN).
Previous status, retained: The catalog latches, sequences and reads velocity off the height of a hit. Nineteen entries, ten of them hand-authored Lua, and the three the use-case survey said to build first are the three that landed: HOLD holds a static value and shows you where it is - self.h is the held cell, an onset or a move repaints it in ONE pass on layers 1 and 2 and arms a sine breathe, a lift resets nothing because that is the whole feature, and the Timer exists for exactly one reason, the 655 s glt ceiling, re-arming every 2560 ms because 256 ticks is a whole phase cycle at every rate the knob offers, so the plan's re-issue of glpfs at phase 0 is invisible rather than a snap once a period. STEPS is the archetypal grid sequencer at the size the pad actually is: a flat 64-cell table, a four-on-the-floor armed in Setup so the card is playing before anyone touches it, a gtt-first Timer that releases the previous column before it plays the new one, and a ninth column and row left dark because eight by eight is what a step sequencer IS. SLAM makes the number of lit rows the velocity byte, keys per-contact state by contact id so two fingers cannot cancel each other, and answers code 9 with its own note-off. Four deviations, every one a measured bug in the specified mechanism rather than a preference: the shipped fixed decay rate of 250 is a phase step of 6 and lands on black at 42 ticks and NOWHERE else - measured freezing at phase 183, 111 and 127 for 12, 24 and 64 - so the rate is derived as 256-252//ticks from a starting phase of 252 with every length a divisor of 252, and after a hit and 400 ticks SLAM's rendered frame is byte-identical to its resting frame; SLAM's bloom owns layer 1 alone because the zone outline is on layer 2 and a decay that ends at exact black would have erased two outline corners per hit forever with no Timer to repaint them; HOLD's clear pass restores the frame colour instead of writing black over it, because the frame and the latch share layer 2 and a latch leaving the border would otherwise punch a permanent hole in it; and SLAM's row count is clamped with glim, because v*3//127+1 is 4 in a three-row zone. The one thing a green test cannot say is said three times: firmware advances prev_* before the writability check and pad-sim.ts cannot manufacture the stuck contact, so a latching configuration can look perfect in a browser and stick on hardware - it is in HOLD's header, in the single-contact half of its card copy, and in row 13 of docs/HARDWARE-AUDITION.md in the document's own voice. The fixture corrected one declaration: SLAM never reports animating at any sampled tick, so its motion is static and the quiet line the plan wrote with the entry is what listing.spec.ts needed. The standing chip row went from nine chips to fourteen, five tags crossing the two-carrier threshold in one wave, and the document that quotes it - which nothing in the repository would have reddened - moved with it. Quick 74 / 780 + 1 todo, unchanged, as an entry wave must be; sweep 4 19 with 354 combinations; svelte-check 550 / 0 / 0; the build unchanged in wall time at nineteen OG images.
Previous status, retained: Adding a configuration to this repository now costs two recorded blocks, one tag array and the three declarations the catalog has always required — never a rewrite of the browse arithmetic. Nineteen literals across filter.spec.ts and sort.spec.ts were sorted by one rule written at the top of both files: a number that is arithmetic over the shipped data is derived, a number that is a review of it stays a literal in one named block, so changing it is a decision somebody made rather than a test somebody silenced. Every derived expectation carries a floor beside it, and the predicate under test is restated in the spec rather than imported, because agreeing with the module is a tautology. NEWEST no longer claims two block sizes: it says the dates run newest first, each date's entries are contiguous, and inside a block the name tie-break decides — true at two dates, three or ten, and observed passing with a third date in the data. PHASE_ADDED_AT is fixed at 2026-09-07 before the first of the twenty is written, so the page shows three blocks rather than nine. copy.spec.ts counts every string a visitor reads instead of reading it: five tests, a corpus assembled by source.kind so the vendored shelf sentence keeps its straight apostrophe without a single id being written down as an exception, and every refusal naming the entry, the field and the code point. The tag vocabulary is gated in both directions against a maintained array, which is the whole mechanism against playble shipping quietly beside playable. The two search anchors the shipped suites lean on — one card for aurora, one for ghost — are protected in the thirty-second run rather than in a hundred-and-twelve-second Playwright run. And the execution gate asks the question it means: a configuration whose whole output is keystrokes is a keyboard, not a silent instrument, while a catalog in which nothing plays is still refused. The HID half of that guarantee is marked at its spot and scheduled for 09-06, because an assertion that cannot be true today is not a gate.

The D-07 gap is closed and the closing is evidence rather than assertion: lua-host.ts exports the fifteen bare globals and the nine self: methods, registerGlobals iterates the first through a Record keyed by it so neither half can drift, and host-surface.spec.ts resolves every call site in every hand-authored entry - at its defaults and at both knob corners, on both events - against that array, refusing anything it cannot account for. The vendored findTraps returned zero hits for a body calling gln and the classifier refused the same body naming it, in one two-sided test, because the compiler's scanner and the browser's host disagree about what the surface is and every published ZONA recipe is written against the half the host does not have. The seven shipped entries passed unedited on the first run. The 283-combination knob sweep has left the parallel quick run for the project whose purpose is anti-drift, by a rename the shipped file-name rule already routed, with nothing it covers trimmed and nineteen references following it. Nothing here authored a configuration, touched listing.ts, front-door.ts or frames.json, or edited a .svelte file.
Previous status, retained: Phase 7 is at its gate, and the gate is green. Everything a machine can check about writing to a ZONA is checked in one run against the bytes that would be deployed: 776 unit tests (73 files), 13 sweep tests, 89 browser tests in two engines at --workers 3 - a scripted navigator.serial and the node suite's own ZONA responder walking every one of the fourteen install states on the probe and the shipped panel, the confirmation, the header lock, the live region and the degrade path, with zero writes without a click counted by class - svelte-check 545 files with 0 errors, all first time, quick with no timeout. The phase's arithmetic reconciles with 07-01's clean-tree baseline exactly (+4 files, +52 tests, sweep unchanged, +12 e2e against the frozen BASE_E2E of 77) and no number was adjusted to fit. SAFE-01 to SAFE-09 and DEGR-02 are closed with the qualifier each earned; SAFE-02, SAFE-03, SAFE-04, SAFE-05 and SAFE-06 each carry a hardware half that is docs/INSTALL-RUNBOOK.md rows D, C, A and F, E, and G, and SAFE-04's key (SERIALNUMBER/FETCH) is source-verified and wire-unproven, so a failed row A closes it as partially verified rather than as a lie. What is NOT done, and is not claimed: the seven-row hardware checklist has been presented to the user and is unanswered. Nobody has written a byte to a real ZONA from this site - the first write is the user's click at row B - and nobody has seen a real module answer for its serial, a RAM try-on survive or not survive a power cycle, a store's read-back take one round or three, 0 ms pacing meet two full-size writes, or the browser's own storage put an original back in a fresh tab. Phase 7's success criteria 1 to 5 are verified-by-user-pending in their (hardware) halves; criterion 5's failure paths close against recorded frames and scripted faults, which is what it asks for. One product finding surfaced while designing row E and is deferred (item 29): a PUT BACK after a keep in an earlier page load is memory-only, because keptThisSession is a field of the store instance and the durable record does not remember that a keep happened.

Previous status, retained: The install store is finished, and the destructive click is a proof rather than a belief. keepOnDevice() sends one PAGESTORE/EXECUTE through the connection's one queue under the descriptor's 3000 ms, then waits for the ZONA's next heartbeat - resolved from the same onClass sink that feeds the queue, because the acknowledgement is sent by the firmware callback that STARTS the module's page reload (grid_decode.c:947-961) and CONFIG/FETCH has no bulk guard - and only then re-fetches both strings, for at most three rounds with retryBackoffMs between them, saying kept on the first byte-identical pair and kept-mismatch when the rounds run out (D-12, D-19, Pitfall 6). The RAM taxonomy is by instanceof on the queue's own classes and by the recorded write-timer step - partial names Timer and Setup, nothing-landed names its cause, lost names the unplug - and a timeout with no NACK anywhere in the action escalates the next queue's pre-send gap to the desktop's 10 ms and rebuilds it (pacingEscalated). Previous status, retained: Every write of this site now has a home, and it is not the session. install.svelte.ts is a second runes store beside session.svelte.ts with exactly three static specifiers (./install-copy and ./snapshot import nothing; ./session.svelte carries Phase 6's four light ones), so the cold load of /c/{id}/ does not grow and test 15's eight needles over the session stay green unedited. At "connected" it bumps a generation, builds ONE RequestQueue over session.transport (the write view) with the injected clock and an onStep that fills `steps`, subscribes session.onClass((cls) => queue.deliver(cls)), and takes the snapshot in the gated order: fetch-serial (a timeout degrades to a session-only copy and never throws), fetchBoth on the module's REPORTED page, canWriteBack (D-03 over Z-16: an empty string is refused, before the record is consulted - deferred item 7), the pair held in memory FIRST, readSnapshot so an existing original WINS over a fresh fetch, persistIfAbsent and rememberLast, then ready and LIVE_SNAPSHOT_SAVED. At "closed" - which also fires with no "connected" before it on the not-zona path - the abort, the unsubscribe and the drop are each guarded on existence, the generation is bumped regardless, and the phase returns to idle only when no leg is in flight while snapshot, moduleId and rememberedModule stay. tryOnDevice and putBack share #ramLeg: writeLock set, writeBoth Timer (6) then Setup (0) verbatim, the generation guard, #classify by instanceof on the awaited transport module's AbortedError / NackError (never message text; an AbortedError is classified BEFORE the generation check, because the closed that bumped it is the event that rejected the waiter), and restorePageChange in a finally on every path with the lock released there. tryOnDevice's refusals are one guard, one reason per line - measuring (undefined), not-writable, no-session, over-budget at CONFIG_MAX read from the awaited protocol module - and from snapshot-failed it retries the snapshot first and writes only if that lands. armed is recomputed (never derived) in observeConfig and after every leg. install.spec.ts 8, in node, with no browser: a tee over FakeTransport (four methods delegated, one callback kept, push() into it, the fake's faults preserved for 07-07), every fake port connected: true so the watchdog stays quiet, the queue's clock stepped in lockstep with fake timers so a 1.3 s retry ladder runs in tens of milliseconds. Previous status, retained: THE ONE NEGATIVE CHECK THAT CAME BACK GREEN IS THE ONE WORTH READING. Selecting the two firmware defaults by ARRAY POSITION instead of by event number turned nothing red: the pinned package happens to declare setup at index 0 and timer at index 1, so the coincidence and the contract agree today and every assertion in the file passes either way. That is exactly why defaultFor() throws at module load on an event the touch element does not declare - the only thing standing between a renumbered event and a write of `undefined` to somebody's module is which lookup was used, and no test can tell you which one you used. - Last 5 plans (10-07 to 10-11): 70, 115, 100, 145, 70 min. 10-11 is back at the phase's median after 10-10's 145, and the split is worth recording because it is the opposite of 10-10's: nothing arrived on disk, nothing had to be reconciled, and the module the plan is named for took minutes - mix.ts is fifty lines. The time went on the three things that are not the feature. A property test that counts its runs, its results and its redrawn positions BEFORE it asserts four empty offender lists. A canvas budget reconciled by re-reading ColourPicker.svelte rather than by citing 10-10's recorded six.
